## Status Summary

- Rows audited in this pass: `591`
- Status counts:
  - `Proved`: `539`
  - `Implemented, proof incomplete`: `0`
  - `Missing`: `0`
  - `Retained Python boundary`: `26`
  - `Intentional divergence`: `11`
  - `Dormant / unproven caller`: `15`
- Audit fixes landed while building this matrix:
  - `internal/projectstate/add_kwarg.go`: missing dict-root initialization now
    preserves the Python-source blank root name instead of inventing `"params"`
  - `internal/httpapi/project_routes.go`: invalid section/code-object/view-mode
    rename cases and invalid window-space name/state cases now return Python-style
    `422 {"detail": ...}` bodies
  - `internal/httpapi/project_routes.go`: invalid initial top-level section names
    now map to `422 {"detail": ...}` instead of falling through to `500`
  - `internal/httpapi/project_shell_routes.go`: `loadProjectConfig` now returns
    a detail-style 404 body, `deleteProjectConfigs` now returns `true`, and
    `deleteNotification` now returns `{"success": bool}` to match the source
  - `internal/deployments/catalog.go` and
    `internal/httpapi/deployment_ide_routes.go`: missing deployment catalog
    directories now once again surface the intended caller contract, so IDE
    deployment `versions` reads return an empty list and `latest` reads return
    typed `404 not_found` instead of falling through to `500 internal_error`
  - `internal/httpapi/project_routes.go`: `prepareSectionsForCopy` no longer
    incorrectly requires deployment `allowParamChanges`; share/app copy now
    reads the active session overlay like the source route
  - `internal/httpapi/listener_routes.go`: `forceRefreshListener` missing-project
    failures now return the Python-style `404 {"detail": ...}` body
  - `internal/wsapi/listener_frontend_emit.go`: missing-project listener emits
    now return the source-style quoted project id in
    `project_state_unavailable.details.message` instead of the broader Go
    runtime error string
  - `internal/mcpx/agentic_context.go`: share-scope UI-setting reveals now keep
    the retained agentic/session frontend affinity authoritative over
    caller-supplied request metadata, and malformed/blank caller affinity tokens
    are ignored instead of failing the reveal path early
  - `internal/httpapi/httpapi.go`, `internal/httpapi/settings_routes.go`, and
    `internal/httpapi/app_definition_routes.go`: malformed JSON on the
    source-typed settings save and direct app-definition HTTP routes now
    return the Python-style `422 {"status_code":10422,"message":...,"data":null}`
    validation envelope instead of the Go-only `400 invalid_json` error
  - `internal/httpapi/settings_routes.go`: missing or null `saveSettings`
    `settings` payloads now return the Python-style
    `422 {"status_code":10422,"message":...,"data":null}` validation
    envelope instead of falling through to the Go-only
    `400 invalid_settings` response
  - `internal/settingsstate/validation.go` and
    `internal/httpapi/settings_routes.go`: `saveSettings` now validates
    typed-invalid nested payloads across the current Go-owned direct-save
    settings branches (`shortcuts`, `gui`, `backend`, `sectionSizingPolicy`,
    and `languageServer`) and returns the Python-style
    `422 {"status_code":10422,"message":...,"data":null}` validation
    envelope before persisting
  - `internal/httpapi/app_definition_routes.go`: missing or null direct
    `validateAppDefinition` / `setAppDefinition` `appSpec` payloads now
    return the Python-style
    `422 {"status_code":10422,"message":...,"data":null}` validation
    envelope instead of reaching the Go runtime as route-level validation
    errors
  - `internal/httpapi/transcription_profile_routes.go`: transcription-profile
    reads now follow the source deployment LLM policy, mutations remain
    IDE-only with source detail bodies, and malformed / missing / out-of-range
    `chunkMs` profile payloads now return the Python-style
    `422 {"status_code":10422,"message":...,"data":null}` validation
    envelope
  - `internal/httpapi/engine_config_routes.go`,
    `internal/realtime/messages.go`, and `internal/app/app.go`:
    engine-config create/update/delete now broadcast source-style
    `EngineConfigsUpdated` full snapshots, including enriched
    `runtimeSupport`, to frontend websocket consumers after each mutation
  - `internal/httpapi/external_agent_config_routes.go`,
    `internal/realtime/messages.go`, and `internal/app/app.go`:
    external-agent-config reads now follow the source deployment LLM policy,
    while create/update/delete once again broadcast
    `ExternalAgentConfigsUpdated` full snapshots to frontend websocket
    consumers after each mutation
  - `internal/app/app.go`, `internal/httpapi/ai_routes.go`, and
    `internal/httpapi/ai_transcription_routes.go`: transcription-session
    routes are no longer incorrectly IDE- or launcher-gated, now follow
    deployment `allowLLM`, malformed / missing request bodies now return the
    Python-style `422 {"status_code":10422,"message":...,"data":null}`
    validation envelope, and omitted `channels` once again defaults to `1`
  - `internal/httpapi/ai_routes.go`: `getAIFollowSnapshot` is no longer
    incorrectly IDE-gated and now follows deployment `allowLLM` for the live
    share/app caller surface
  - `internal/httpapi/terminal_routes.go`: deployment `runCode`
    share-session failures now return the source `400/404 {"detail": ...}`
    bodies instead of inheriting the generic `missing_share_session` /
    `unknown_share_session` error envelope from the shared deployment
    middleware
  - `internal/httpapi/terminal_routes.go`: malformed `runCode` JSON now
    returns the Python/FastAPI-style
    `422 {"status_code":10422,"message":...,"data":null}` validation
    envelope instead of the Go-only `400 invalid_json` error
  - `internal/httpapi/filebrowser_routes.go`: deployment
    `checkIfFileExists` now mirrors the source allowlist by resolving paths
    relative to the deployment root and allowing only `artifacts`,
    `json_sources`, and `code` subtrees instead of blocking the route
    wholesale in deployment mode
  - `internal/mcpx/server.go`, `internal/mcpx/ui_setting_tools.go`, and
    `internal/mcpx/agentic_context.go`: `set_ui_setting_value` now exposes the
    Python-style `revealAfterSet` write-plus-reveal contract with nested reveal
    results, and allow-unscoped agentic context keeps structural write access
    even when section context is present for the reveal path
  - `internal/plugins/discovery.go`: persisted non-object plugin config roots
    now invalidate the plugin before runtime/UI-setting exposure, so MCP
    UI-setting reads and writes fail closed without rewriting the corrupt root
  - `internal/wsapi/routes.go`: listener init missing-project error payloads now
    omit the extra Go-only `"message"` field so the retained websocket transport
    matches the source contract
  - `internal/httpapi/project_routes.go` and `internal/httpapi/lsp_routes.go`:
    `setTypeErrorIgnore` now lives on the project-mutation route stack so IDE
    callers keep the existing behavior and deployment/share callers follow the
    source param-change policy and overlay semantics
  - `internal/httpapi/lsp_routes.go`: LSP admin and project-manager route
    mode guards now return the Python-style `403 {"detail": ...}` bodies,
    including the source-specific deployment messages for full-runtime versus
    language-server feature routes
  - `internal/httpapi/agent_profile_routes.go`: agent-profile mutations and
    missing-profile failures now return the source `detail` bodies for
    share-mode and `404` errors instead of the generic Go mode/error envelopes
  - `internal/wsapi/routes.go`: deployment terminal websocket writes now
    ignore blocked input and keep the read-only terminal session attached
    instead of closing the socket on `ErrInputDisabled`
  - `internal/wsapi/wsapi.go` and `internal/wsapi/routes.go`: terminal
    share-session and deployment-policy websocket failures now close with the
    source code-only contracts again, omitting the Go-only reason strings on
    `4400`, `4404`, and `4403` terminal close paths
  - `internal/wsapi/routes_test.go`: listener websocket route coverage now
    locks live realtime `dependencyValues` transport, dependency-closure
    delivery, and temporary-id translation for retained listener clients
  - `internal/gitread/service.go`: git status responses now include the
    source-style `worktrees` list again, including current/prunable metadata,
    and the generated frontend bindings expose that contract to the git pane
  - `internal/uisettings/plugins.go` and `internal/uisettings/registry.go`:
    plugin UI-setting registry construction now degrades duplicate/invalid
    plugin setting entries per setting id instead of failing the whole plugin
    registry, so affected setting ids return `plugin_unavailable` while healthy
    sibling settings remain available
  - `internal/httpapi/settings_routes.go`: settings load/save mode guards now
    return the Python-style `{"detail": ...}` body, and `saveSettings`
    preserves the source share-mode-specific forbidden message
  - `internal/httpapi/app_definition_routes.go`: `getAppCatalog` is no longer
    IDE-gated, app-definition HTTP routes now block only deployment modes with
    the source structural-change detail, launcher requests can succeed when a
    project runtime exists, and missing-manager / invalid-set failures now use
    source-style `404` / `422` detail bodies
  - `internal/httpapi/prompt_template_routes.go`: prompt-template list/get now
    follow the Python source deployment read policy, staying available in
    share/app modes when LLM access is allowed while prompt-template mutations
    remain IDE-only
  - `internal/transcriptionsessions/service.go` and
    `internal/transcriptionsessions/realtime_engine.go`: auto-mode
    transcription now retries file mode for bounded realtime-capability setup
    failures instead of surfacing those startup failures as terminal errors
  - `internal/plugins/codegen_generation.go`,
    `internal/codegen/plugin_materializer.go`, and `internal/app/app.go`:
    plugin backend codegen targets now materialize on persisted main-runtime
    save, canonicalize relative output paths, reject absolute or escaping
    output paths, and reuse cached outputs only until missing/modified/extra
    outputs or implementation-key changes require regeneration
  - route tests were updated to lock those transport contracts

## Scope Notes

- This pass is intentionally limited to the cluster named in the plan:
  project names, view-mode names, window-space names, section names, param
  names, generated/default names on create flows, related rename/create error
  contracts, and websocket name-validation broadcasts.
- This pass also extends the matrix into the next suggested bounded slice:
  project-shell metadata/load/save/save-as/default-project/delete-project and
  notifications behavior.
- This pass also extends the matrix into the listener/websocket slice:
  frontend identity, listener force-refresh transport, listener coverage
  snapshots, listener bootstrap/register/frontend-emit error contracts, and
  plugin listener endpoint transport.
- This pass also extends the matrix into the terminals/run-code slice:
  terminal websocket attach/history/resize/read-only behavior, share-scoped
  delete and interrupt behavior, attached-terminal selection, generated-entry
  terminal allocation/reuse, run overrides, and deployment run-policy/session
  overlay rules.
- This pass also extends the matrix into the detached-run slice: route
  launcher/deployment/snapshot validation, override validation, detached-run
  snapshot planning, and detached-run manager lifecycle/status persistence.
- This pass also extends the matrix into the first format-code/watch-code/LSP
  slice: format-code target validation and sanitized errors, watch-code
  baseline/update-order/create-delete behavior, and LSP
  diagnostics/completion/restart/type-check/type-error-ignore route contracts.
- This pass also extends the matrix into a targeted watch-code/LSP follow-up
  slice: watch-code move/rename/signature lifecycle behavior, LSP
  plugin-language type-check refresh, and LSP mode-guard detail-body parity
  for admin and project-manager route families.

- This pass also extends the matrix into a targeted terminal / run-code /

  surface instead of preserving the prior `0 Missing` claim.

  the shared child-create validator now admits the source-supported
  `GlobalVariables` / `GenPythonCode` / `GenPythonMetadata` family on both
  direct and incrementer-backed creation, while persisted
  `defaultChildSectionType` correctly remains the narrower source family.

  the section-create family re-audit and follow-on control-type proof pass.

  the checkout still does not carry a full generated Go mirror of every legacy
  historical settings branch, so save-time validation is now proved for the

  in `setta-settings.json`.
